# Codex conversation 01a0a5e8-ff60-7b41-88e0-0879738408bf

## assistant

<proposed_plan>
# LSAT U booking confirmation upgrade

## Summary

Show confirmation immediately after payment, then update it automatically when Google finishes creating and sending the invitation. Replace the sparse page with a Calendly-inspired LSAT U confirmation card, including the requested corner ribbon and a calendar-only action.

## Implementation changes

- Start from the latest remote `main`, preserving the existing dirty local branch and Lovable’s recent changes.
- Expand the protected booking-status response with:
  - Attendee name
  - Session title
  - Start and end time
  - Duration
  - Booking timezone
  - Calendar readiness
  - Google Calendar event URL
- Persist the timezone selected during booking. Existing bookings fall back to the browser timezone, then `America/Toronto`.
- Show the final confirmation page immediately:
  - Initially: “You are scheduled!” and “We’re preparing your calendar invitation.”
  - Continue polling while Google Calendar finishes.
  - Once ready: “A calendar invitation has been sent to your email address.”
  - Enable one primary “Open Invitation” button linked to the verified Google Calendar event.
  - If preparation is delayed, keep the booking confirmed and show a truthful recovery message instead of spinning indefinitely.
- Build the meeting-details card with the session title, attendee name, localized date and time, duration, timezone, and a note that Google Meet details are included in the calendar invitation.
- Add a diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” corner ribbon. Do not reproduce Calendly trademarks, assets, menu, or copy-link controls.
- Keep the confirmation page calendar-only, with no separate Meet button.
- Correct the email wording to past tense: the invitation “has been sent,” not “is on its way.” Retain the useful Meet action in the email.
- Replace the hardcoded 70-minute email duration and Toronto timezone with values from the booking.
- Update the outbox worker to perform a bounded second claim after calendar creation, allowing newly unblocked confirmation emails to send during the same webhook-triggered run instead of waiting up to one minute.
- Preserve the required ordering: payment finalized, calendar event and Meet created, Google invitation sent, confirmation email sent. Do not claim that Google has placed the event directly onto every recipient’s calendar because recipient settings can still require acceptance.

## Public interface changes

- Extend the capability-protected status payload with `attendee_name`, `event_label`, `duration_minutes`, `timezone`, `calendar_ready`, and `calendar_url`.
- Add a validated IANA timezone to the booking hold/finalization data.
- Resolve `calendar_url` from the mirrored Google event only after validating the booking capability token.

## Test and acceptance plan

- Test immediate booked rendering followed by automatic calendar-ready updating.
- Test calendar button enablement, delayed-calendar recovery, expired tokens, and unauthorized status requests.
- Test dynamic session titles, durations, timezones, and daylight-saving formatting.
- Test that Google promotion completes before email eligibility and that the same worker invocation drains newly unblocked email jobs without duplicates.
- Render-test the email to confirm the corrected wording and accurate booking details.
- Browser-test narrow, medium, wide, and side-panel layouts, including ribbon placement, keyboard access, focus states, and screen-reader labels.
- After deployment, run one separately authorized $1 live booking and verify the Stripe payment, confirmation page, Google invitation, Meet details, and confirmation email end to end.

## Assumptions

- The requested “automatic” behavior means LSAT U sends the invitation immediately. Google still controls whether an external invitation appears automatically or awaits acceptance.
- Deploying the Edge Function through Lovable requires Joshua’s explicit confirmation immediately before any action that may spend Lovable credits.
- No real payment or Lovable credit will be used during implementation without that fresh confirmation.
</proposed_plan>

<proposed_plan>
# Match the Calendly confirmation experience with LSAT U branding

## Summary

Use the supplied screenshot as the visual source of truth for both the confirmation email and the booking-success page. Preserve its restrained layout and information hierarchy, replacing every Calendly element with LSAT U branding and real booking data.

## Confirmation email

- Send from `LSAT U <noreply@lsatprep.study>`.
- Replace the current decorative gold confirmation card with the screenshot’s clean, white, centered email layout.
- Reproduce the visible structure:
  - Centered LSAT U logo
  - Light dotted divider
  - Left-aligned content column
  - `Hi [first name],`
  - `Your LSAT U session has been scheduled.`
  - Bold field labels with values beneath them
  - Event type
  - Invitee name
  - Invitee email
  - Date and time
  - Duration
  - Timezone
  - Location: Google Meet
- Use LSAT U’s existing typography and colors while matching the screenshot’s generous whitespace, narrow content width, light dividers, and minimal styling.
- Keep a Google Meet button lower in the email, followed by plain fallback links for clients that block styled buttons.
- Change the inaccurate sentence to: “Your Google Calendar invitation has been sent.”
- Remove “is on its way” and do not imply that the LSAT U confirmation email is waiting for calendar creation.
- Populate duration and timezone from the booking instead of hardcoding 70 minutes and Toronto.
- Build with email-safe tables and inline styles so Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, and mobile clients remain visually consistent.

## Gmail event panel

- Continue creating the real Google Calendar event with the invitee as an attendee, Google Meet attached, and `sendUpdates: "all"`.
- Add validated `EventReservation` structured metadata to the LSAT U confirmation email with the event title, attendee, start time, location, reservation number, and secure reservation URL.
- Add a Gmail `ViewAction` targeting the booking’s protected reservation page.
- Validate the markup with Google’s Email Markup Tester.
- Prepare the required Google sender-registration submission using the production sender, matching SPF or DKIM, `From`, and return-path domains. Google requires registration before marked-up production emails reliably receive enhanced Gmail treatment. [Google registration requirements](https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/registering-with-google)
- Treat Gmail’s blue event panel as an enhancement, not part of the HTML email. Gmail controls its exact appearance, labels, conflict warnings, and available buttons. LSAT U can provide the correct event metadata but cannot force Gmail to render an identical panel. [EventReservation specification](https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/types/EventReservation)

## Confirmation page

- Show “You are scheduled!” immediately after payment confirmation.
- Mirror the earlier Calendly confirmation-page structure:
  - Sent-calendar-invitation message
  - Calendar-only primary action
  - Session-details panel
  - Attendee name
  - Localized date and time
  - Duration
  - Timezone
  - Google Meet availability note
  - Diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” corner ribbon
- While Google is still creating the event, show “Preparing your calendar invitation” and keep the calendar action disabled.
- Continue polling after the booking becomes `booked`.
- Replace the preparing state automatically with “A calendar invitation has been sent to your email address” and enable “Open Invitation” when the Google event URL becomes available.
- If Google is delayed, keep the booking confirmed and display a recovery message instead of spinning indefinitely.

## Backend and interface changes

- Persist the timezone selected during booking.
- Extend the capability-protected status response with `attendee_name`, `event_label`, `duration_minutes`, `timezone`, `calendar_ready`, and `calendar_url`.
- Resolve `calendar_url` from the mirrored Google event only after validating the booking token.
- Make the outbox worker claim newly unblocked confirmation-email jobs again after calendar promotion, so the email can send in the same webhook-triggered run.
- Preserve idempotency and the required ordering: payment finalized, calendar and Meet created, Google invitation sent, confirmation email sent.

## Verification

- Snapshot-test the email at desktop and mobile widths against the supplied reference.
- Render-test Gmail, Outlook, and Apple Mail output.
- Test real booking values, long names, different durations, multiple timezones, daylight-saving transitions, and missing optional notes.
- Test the immediate confirmation page, calendar-preparing transition, successful calendar link, delayed-calendar recovery, and invalid booking tokens.
- Verify the structured metadata with Google’s official validator.
- After deployment, perform one separately authorized $1 production booking and verify Stripe payment, calendar invitation, Meet link, Gmail presentation, confirmation page, and LSAT U email.
- Lovable deployment or any action that may consume Lovable credits requires Joshua’s fresh confirmation immediately before execution.

## Assumptions

- “Exactly” means faithfully reproducing the reference email’s layout and hierarchy with LSAT U branding and booking-specific content.
- Gmail’s surrounding interface and generated blue event panel cannot be pixel-controlled by the email template.
- Google may still require the recipient to accept an external invitation depending on that recipient’s Calendar settings.
</proposed_plan>

# LSAT U burgundy booking confirmation

## Summary

Match the supplied Calendly reference using LSAT U branding and its existing burgundy palette. The email body and confirmation page will use burgundy instead of blue. Gmail’s generated event panel may remain blue because Google controls that interface.

## Visual specification

- Use LSAT U’s production booking color:
  - Primary: `hsl(347 70% 27%)`, approximately `#75152A`
  - Dark gradient: `#4F0D1C → #6E1423 → #8E1D3A`
  - White button and ribbon text
- Use exact inline hex colors in email HTML because email clients cannot access the website’s CSS variables.
- Preserve the reference’s white background, generous spacing, narrow content column, understated dividers, and simple typography.
- Replace the blue Calendly logo with the LSAT U mark in burgundy.
- Use burgundy for buttons, links, important icons, and the “Powered by LSAT U” ribbon.
- Do not make the entire email burgundy or reproduce any Calendly branding.

## Confirmation email

- Recreate the reference structure:
  - Centered burgundy LSAT U logo
  - Light divider
  - `Hi [first name],`
  - `Your LSAT U session has been scheduled.`
  - Event type
  - Invitee name and email
  - Date, time, duration, and timezone
  - Google Meet location
- Change the outdated message to: “Your Google Calendar invitation has been sent.”
- Keep a burgundy Google Meet button and plain fallback link.
- Use booking-derived duration and timezone instead of hardcoded values.
- Build with email-safe tables and inline styles for Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, and mobile clients.

## Gmail event panel

- Continue sending the actual Google Calendar invitation automatically with the attendee and Meet link.
- Add validated `EventReservation` metadata and a secure reservation action.
- Register the production sender with Google if LSAT U qualifies for enhanced Gmail markup.
- Accept that Gmail controls the generated panel’s blue background, button labels, conflict warnings, and final appearance. LSAT U cannot recolor Gmail’s own interface.

## Confirmation page

- Show “You are scheduled!” immediately after payment.
- Use the same burgundy palette for the success icon, calendar button, links, borders, and diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” ribbon.
- Display attendee, session type, date, time, duration, timezone, and conferencing information.
- Provide one calendar-only “Open Invitation” action.
- Continue polling while the calendar invitation is prepared, then enable the button automatically.
- Show a recoverable confirmed state if Google Calendar is delayed.

## Backend and verification

- Persist the selected timezone.
- Extend the protected booking-status response with the display details, calendar readiness, and calendar URL.
- Allow the outbox worker to send newly unblocked confirmation emails during the same run after calendar creation.
- Preserve payment, calendar, invitation, and email idempotency.
- Compare desktop and mobile email renders against the supplied screenshot.
- Test Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, different durations and timezones, delayed calendar creation, and invalid booking tokens.
- Perform one separately authorized $1 production booking after deployment.
- Obtain fresh confirmation before any Lovable action that could consume credits.

## Assumptions

- “Instead of blue” applies to all LSAT U-controlled email and confirmation-page elements.
- Gmail’s surrounding interface and generated event panel are excluded because their colors cannot be controlled by LSAT U.
</proposed_plan>

Use the absolute paths above.\n\n## Project Map\n\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\n\n## Stack And Commands\n\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\n- Build: `npm run build`\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\n- Test: `npm run test`\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\n\n## Working Rules\n\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\n\n## Product Constraints\n\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\n\n## Smart Drill Note\n\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\n\n## Verification (must not skip)\n\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\n\n## Workflow Expectations\n\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\n\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\n\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\n\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\n\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong "Done" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\n\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\n\n## Codex Adapter Layout\n\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\n\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\n\n## Known Gaps\n\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\n\n## Agent skills\n\n### Issue tracker\n\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n\n### Triage labels\n\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\n\n### Domain docs\n\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\n\r\n'}]

text,\r\n  guests jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,\r\n  client_idempotency_key text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  draft_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  checkout_request_fingerprint text,\r\n  checkout_fingerprint_disposition text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none',\r\n  stripe_checkout_session_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_checkout_url text,\r\n  stripe_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_payment_intent_id text,\r\n  amount_cents integer,\r\n  currency text,\r\n  livemode boolean,\r\n  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\r\n  booking_token_hash text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  booking_token_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  cancel_token_hash text UNIQUE,\r\n  cancel_token_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_refund_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_refund_status text,\r\n  gcal_event_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  meet_url text,\r\n  lifecycle_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',\r\n  status_changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  terminal_reason text,\r\n  ip_hash text,\r\n  sweep_worker_id text,\r\n  sweep_lease_token uuid,\r\n  sweep_lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  sweep_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_duration_chk CHECK (end_at = start_at + interval '70 minutes'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_guests_chk CHECK (jsonb_typeof(guests) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(guests) <= 10),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_lifecycle_version_chk CHECK (lifecycle_version >= 0),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_chk CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents = 4500),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_currency_chk CHECK (currency IS NULL OR lower(currency) = 'usd'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_status_chk CHECK (status IN (\r\n    'draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked', 'cancelled', 'expired', 'abandoned',\r\n    'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_fingerprint_disposition_chk CHECK (checkout_fingerprint_disposition IN (\r\n    'none', 'persisted', 'attached', 'stripe_expired', 'stripe_absent'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_checkout_expiry_chk CHECK (\r\n    (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NOT NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NOT NULL)\r\n  ),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_cancel_token_chk CHECK (\r\n    (cancel_token_hash IS NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (cancel_token_hash IS NOT NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at = start_at)\r\n  )\r\n);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_no_occupying_overlap\r\n  EXCLUDE USING gist (tstzrange(start_at, end_at, '[)') WITH &&)\r\n  WHERE (status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked'));\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_start_at_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (start_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_status_expiry_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (status, stripe_expires_at, draft_expires_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_email_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (lower(email)) WHERE email IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_user_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (user_id, start_at) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_sweep_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (sweep_lease_until, status, updated_at);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.single_session_outbox (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  booking_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.single_session_bookings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\r\n  action text NOT NULL,\r\n  recipient text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',\r\n  state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',\r\n  attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  max_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 8,\r\n  lease_worker_id text,\r\n  lease_token uuid,\r\n  lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  next_retry_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  provider_status text,\r\n  last_error text,\r\n  idempotency_key text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  precondition_status text NOT NULL,\r\n  precondition_lifecycle_version integer NOT NULL,\r\n  payload_ciphertext text,\r\n  result jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\r\n  sensitive_payload_purged_at timestamptz,\r\n  completed_at timestamptz,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_outbox_action_chk CHECK (action IN (\r\n    'gcal_promote', 'gcal_delete', 'email_booking_confirmation',\r\n    'email_admin_notification', 'email_cancellation',\r\n    'email_refund_apology', 'stripe_refund'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_outbox_state_chk CHECK (state IN (\r\n    'blocked', 'pending', 'leased', 'succeeded', 'failed', 'superseded'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_outbox_attempts_chk CHECK (\r\n    attempts >= 0 AND max_attempts > 0 AND attempts <= max_attempts\r\n  ),\r\n  UNIQUE (booking_id, action, recipient)\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_outbox_claim_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_outbox (state, next_retry_at, lease_until, created_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_outbox_booking_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_outbox (booking_id, state);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.booking_worker_heartbeats (\r\n  worker_name text PRIMARY KEY,\r\n  last_start_at timestamptz,\r\n  last_success_at timestamptz,\r\n  last_error_at timestamptz,\r\n  last_error text,\r\n  metadata jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  CONSTRAINT booking_worker_name_chk CHECK (worker_name IN (\r\n    'single-session-sweeper', 'single-session-outbox', 'gcal-reconciler'\r\n  ))\r\n);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_outbox ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_worker_heartbeats ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON public.single_session_bookings FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nREVOKE ALL ON public.single_session_outbox FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nREVOKE ALL ON public.booking_worker_heartbeats FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.single_session_bookings TO service_role;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.single_session_outbox TO service_role;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_worker_heartbeats TO service_role;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enforce_single_session_lifecycle()\r\nRETURNS trigger\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  NEW.updated_at := now();\r\n\r\n  IF NEW.status = OLD.status THEN\r\n    IF NEW.lifecycle_version <> OLD.lifecycle_version THEN\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'lifecycle_version_without_transition' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n    END IF;\r\n    NEW.status_changed_at := OLD.status_changed_at;\r\n    RETURN NEW;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NEW.lifecycle_version <> OLD.lifecycle_version + 1 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_lifecycle_version' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NOT (\r\n    (OLD.status = 'draft' AND NEW.status IN ('checkout_open', 'expired')) OR\r\n    (OLD.status = 'checkout_open' AND NEW.status IN ('booked', 'expired', 'abandoned', 'refund_pending')) OR\r\n    (OLD.status = 'expired' AND NEW.status IN ('booked', 'refund_pending')) OR\r\n    (OLD.status = 'abandoned' AND NEW.status = 'refund_pending') OR\r\n    (OLD.status = 'booked' AND NEW.status = 'cancelled') OR\r\n    (OLD.status = 'refund_pending' AND NEW.status IN ('refunded', 'refund_failed')) OR\r\n    (OLD.status = 'refund_failed' AND NEW.status = 'refunded')\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_single_session_transition:%->%', OLD.status, NEW.status\r\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- What a bookable event IS, as data instead of as constants.\r\n--\r\n-- The single session\'s shape -- 70 minutes, 24 hours\' notice, no buffers, a six\r\n-- business day window, $45 -- is currently written into the edge function AND\r\n-- into three SQL functions that enforce the slot policy inside the booking\r\n-- transaction. That is why there can only ever be one kind of session: a\r\n-- consultation is 30 minutes with 30-minute buffers and a five day window, and\r\n-- there is nowhere to say so.\r\n--\r\n-- This migration only creates the vocabulary and seeds it with the values that\r\n-- are live today. Nothing reads it yet, so this changes no behavior. The SQL\r\n-- policy functions are switched over to it next, one at a time.\r\n--\r\n-- Every number here was read from Joshua\'s Cal.com account on 2026-08-30, which\r\n-- stays the reference for what these events are supposed to be.\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_event_types (\r\n  slug text PRIMARY KEY,\r\n  label text NOT NULL,\r\n  length_minutes int NOT NULL CHECK (length_minutes > 0),\r\n  -- Null means "start a slot every length_minutes", which is what Cal.com does\r\n  -- when no explicit interval is set. The single session\'s back-to-back 70\r\n  -- minute chain (1:00, 2:10, 3:20) is this rule, not a quirk.\r\n  slot_interval_minutes int CHECK (slot_interval_minutes IS NULL OR slot_interval_minutes > 0),\r\n  minimum_notice_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440 CHECK (minimum_notice_minutes >= 0),\r\n  buffer_before_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_before_minutes >= 0),\r\n  buffer_after_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_after_minutes >= 0),\r\n  -- Business days size the window; days inside it are offered whenever the\r\n  -- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.\r\n  window_business_days int NOT NULL DEFAULT 6 CHECK (window_business_days > 0),\r\n  price_cents int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\r\n  currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'usd\',\r\n  requires_payment boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\r\n  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\n\r\nINSERT INTO public.booking_event_types (\r\n  slug, label, length_minutes, slot_interval_minutes, minimum_notice_minutes,\r\n  buffer_before_minutes, buffer_after_minutes, window_business_days,\r\n  price_cents, currency, requires_payment\r\n) VALUES\r\n  (\'single-session\', \'LSAT Session\', 70, NULL, 1440, 0,  0,  6, 4500, \'usd\', true),\r\n  (\'consultation\',   \'Consultation\', 30, NULL, 1440, 30, 30, 5,    0, \'usd\', false)\r\nON CONFLICT (slug) DO NOTHING;\r\n\r\n-- One student, one price. Joshua already runs this by hand on Cal.com -- there\r\n-- are twelve event types on that account and most of them exist only because a\r\n-- particular person pays a particular rate. Keyed by email because the booker\r\n-- is not necessarily signed in when they book.\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_price_overrides (\r\n  email text NOT NULL,\r\n  event_slug text NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE,\r\n  price_cents int NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\r\n  note text,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  PRIMARY KEY (email, event_slug)\r\n);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_price_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\n\r\n-- Students never read these directly: the booking page gets everything it needs\r\n-- from the edge function, which runs as service_role.\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking event types"\r\n  ON public.booking_event_types FOR ALL TO authenticated\r\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\r\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking price overrides"\r\n  ON public.booking_price_overrides FOR ALL TO authenticated\r\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\r\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_event_types TO authenticated;\r\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_price_overrides TO authenticated;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_event_types TO service_role;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_price_overrides TO service_role;\r\n\r\n-- The price a given booker pays for a given event: their override, else the\r\n-- event\'s own price. One definition, so the checkout and the payment\r\n-- verification can never disagree about what was owed.\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(_event_slug text, _email text)\r\nRETURNS integer\r\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\n  SELECT COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT o.price_cents FROM public.booking_price_overrides o\r\n      WHERE o.event_slug = _event_slug AND lower(o.email) = lower(COALESCE(_email, \'\'))),\r\n    (SELECT t.price_cents FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _event_slug)\r\n  );\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) TO service_role;\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- One-off booking links: a single-use, single-price bookable session.\r\n--\r\n-- Joshua sends someone a URL, they book one session at a price set for them,\r\n-- and the link stops working. This needs no new booking machinery: an event type\r\n-- already carries length, notice, buffers, window and price, and every layer\r\n-- (page, edge function, slot policy, claim transaction) is already keyed by\r\n-- event slug. A one-off link IS a booking_event_types row with a random slug and\r\n-- single_use set.\r\n--\r\n-- Two things stood in the way and are fixed here.\r\n--\r\n-- 1. `single_session_bookings` still carried CHECK constraints from the day\r\n--    there was exactly one sellable thing: `end_at = start_at + 70 minutes` and\r\n--    `amount_cents = 4500`. Those made a custom-length or custom-price event\r\n--    impossible to insert. They also mean the already-seeded 30-minute free\r\n--    `consultation` event cannot currently be booked at all -- its insert\r\n--    violates the 70-minute check. Replacing them with a trigger that reads the\r\n--    event\'s own length fixes both.\r\n--\r\n-- 2. Nothing enforced single use. The same trigger does it, under a per-slug\r\n--    advisory lock so two people clicking the same link at the same moment\r\n--    cannot both get through.\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- The link itself\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS single_use boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pinned_start_at timestamptz,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS note text;\r\n\r\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.single_use IS\r\n  \'A one-off link: once a booking against this slug is held or paid, the slug is spent and no further booking may be claimed.\';\r\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.pinned_start_at IS\r\n  \'One exact start time this link sells. When set, the link offers only this time and the notice, window and weekly-hours rules do not apply -- Joshua already decided this time is fine. Double-booking is still refused by the claim transaction.\';\r\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.note IS\r\n  \'Admin-only reminder of who a one-off link was made for. Never sent to the browser.\';\r\n\r\n-- A pinned time is a one-off idea: it names a single instant, so it can only be\r\n-- sold once.\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\r\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS booking_event_types_pinned_is_single_use_chk;\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT booking_event_types_pinned_is_single_use_chk\r\n  CHECK (pinned_start_at IS NULL OR single_use);\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- Duration and price stop being constants\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_duration_chk,\r\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_amount_chk;\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_amount_nonnegative_chk;\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_nonnegative_chk\r\n  CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents >= 0);\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- What the dropped CHECKs used to say, said against the event instead\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape()\r\nRETURNS trigger\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _event record;\r\n  _spent integer;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event\r\n  FROM public.booking_event_types\r\n  WHERE slug = NEW.event_slug;\r\n\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_event\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  -- The length is the event\'s, not the caller\'s. This is the old 70-minute\r\n  -- CHECK, generalised: a consultation is 30 minutes and a one-off link is\r\n  -- whatever it was minted as.\r\n  IF NEW.end_at <> NEW.start_at + make_interval(mins => _event.length_minutes) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'duration_mismatch\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _event.single_use THEN\r\n    -- Serialise every claim against this one link, so a simultaneous pair of\r\n    -- clicks cannot both read "unspent" and both insert. Taken before the read,\r\n    -- released at transaction end.\r\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(\'one-off-booking-link:\' || NEW.event_slug));\r\n\r\n    -- Spent means a booking exists that is live or was paid. A hold holds the\r\n    -- link (so nobody else can start one while a checkout is open); expiring or\r\n    -- abandoning that hold releases it again. A cancelled or refunded booking\r\n    -- stays spent -- the session was sold once, and a replacement gets a\r\n    -- replacement link.\r\n    SELECT count(*) INTO _spent\r\n    FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n    WHERE b.event_slug = NEW.event_slug\r\n      AND b.status NOT IN (\'expired\', \'abandoned\')\r\n      -- A retry of the same request is the same booking, not a second one.\r\n      AND b.client_idempotency_key <> NEW.client_idempotency_key;\r\n\r\n    IF _spent > 0 THEN\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION \'link_already_used\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0410\';\r\n    END IF;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  RETURN NEW;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS enforce_booking_event_shape ON public.single_session_bookings;\r\nCREATE TRIGGER enforce_booking_event_shape\r\n  BEFORE INSERT ON public.single_session_bookings\r\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape();\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape() FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- Is a one-off link still open? Read by the booking page before it offers times.\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(_event_slug text)\r\nRETURNS boolean\r\nLANGUAGE sql\r\nSTABLE\r\nSECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\n  SELECT EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1\r\n    FROM public.booking_event_types t\r\n    JOIN public.single_session_bookings b ON b.event_slug = t.slug\r\n    WHERE t.slug = _event_slug\r\n      AND t.single_use\r\n      AND b.status NOT IN (\'expired\', \'abandoned\')\r\n  );\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(text) TO service_role;\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- A pinned link sells one instant, and only that instant\r\n--\r\n-- Everything this function normally asks -- is it far enough out, is it inside\r\n-- the rolling window, does it fall inside the weekly hours and on the slot grid\r\n-- -- exists to decide whether a time is one Joshua would agree to. A pinned\r\n-- link is that agreement, made in advance and by hand: he picked the time and\r\n-- sent it to one person. So the questions are answered, and the only thing left\r\n-- to check is that the caller is asking for the time on the link.\r\n--\r\n-- This does NOT relax the double-booking check. That lives in\r\n-- claim_single_session_slot, runs inside the booking transaction, and still\r\n-- refuses a slot that overlaps another booking or a calendar event.\r\n--\r\n-- Links without a pinned time keep the ordinary policy, reading their own\r\n-- minimum_notice_minutes and window_business_days -- which is how a short-notice\r\n-- link is made: mint it with a notice of zero.\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n  _start_at timestamptz,\r\n  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\r\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'\r\n) RETURNS boolean\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    \'America/Toronto\'\r\n  );\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer;\r\n  _pitch integer;\r\n  _local_start timestamp;\r\n  _local_date date;\r\n  _local_time time;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n\r\n  -- The pinned instant, and nothing else. Still refuse to sell one in the past.\r\n  IF _event.pinned_start_at IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n    RETURN _start_at = _event.pinned_start_at AND _start_at > _now;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\r\n  -- No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is what\r\n  -- Cal.com does and what produces the session\'s 1:00 / 2:10 / 3:20 chain.\r\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\r\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\r\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\r\n\r\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\r\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  RETURN EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1\r\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\r\n    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer\r\n      AND _local_time >= a.start_time\r\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\r\n      AND mod(\r\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\r\n        _pitch\r\n      ) = 0\r\n  );\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\r\n  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.\r\n  RETURN false;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) TO service_role;\r\n'}]

RETURNING id INTO _new_id;\r\n  EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN\r\n    SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n      WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\r\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\r\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\r\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\r\n  END;\r\n  UPDATE public.booking_passes bp SET\r\n    used_credits = bp.used_credits + 1,\r\n    status = CASE WHEN bp.used_credits + 1 >= bp.total_credits THEN \'exhausted\' ELSE \'active\' END\r\n  WHERE bp.id = _pass_id RETURNING * INTO _pass;\r\n  redemption_id := _new_id; start_at := _start_at; end_at := _end_at;\r\n  used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\r\n  status := _pass.status; was_existing := false; RETURN NEXT;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 6. single-session policy\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS window_calendar_days integer;\r\n\r\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types\r\nSET buffer_before_minutes = 10, buffer_after_minutes = 10,\r\n    slot_interval_minutes = 80, window_calendar_days = 8, updated_at = now()\r\nWHERE slug = \'single-session\';\r\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types\r\nSET window_calendar_days = 8, updated_at = now()\r\nWHERE slug = \'consultation\';\r\n\r\n-- Window end for an event: calendar days when set (Cal.com "calendarDays",\r\n-- rolling), else the business-day count it always had.\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'::text)\r\nRETURNS date\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\r\nSET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    \'America/Toronto\'\r\n  );\r\n  _day date := (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _target integer;\r\n  _business_days integer := 0;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\r\n  IF FOUND AND _event.window_calendar_days IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n    RETURN _day + _event.window_calendar_days;\r\n  END IF;\r\n  _target := COALESCE(_event.window_business_days, 6);\r\n  WHILE _business_days < _target LOOP\r\n    IF extract(isodow FROM _day) BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN\r\n      _business_days := _business_days + 1;\r\n    END IF;\r\n    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;\r\n  END LOOP;\r\n  RETURN _day;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'::text)\r\nRETURNS boolean\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\r\nSET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    \'America/Toronto\'\r\n  );\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer;\r\n  _pitch integer;\r\n  _local_start timestamp;\r\n  _local_date date;\r\n  _local_time time;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _event.pinned_start_at IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n    RETURN _start_at = _event.pinned_start_at AND _start_at > _now;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\r\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\r\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\r\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\r\n\r\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\r\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  RETURN EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1\r\n    FROM public.booking_ranges_for_date(_local_date) a\r\n    WHERE _local_time >= a.start_time\r\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\r\n      AND mod(\r\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\r\n        _pitch\r\n      ) = 0\r\n  );\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\r\n  RETURN false;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(_start_at timestamptz, _name text, _email text, _notes text, _guests jsonb, _client_idempotency_key text, _booking_token_hash text, _ip_hash text, _user_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'::text)\r\nRETURNS single_session_bookings\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer;\r\n  _end_at timestamptz;\r\n  _email_normalized text := lower(trim(_email));\r\n  _guests_normalized jsonb;\r\n  _expired record;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\r\n  _length := COALESCE(_event.length_minutes, 70);\r\n  _end_at := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _length);\r\n\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\r\n  IF FOUND THEN\r\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_booking.start_at));\r\n    SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking.id FOR UPDATE;\r\n    RETURN _booking;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at, now(), _event_slug) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot_unavailable\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0425\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF NULLIF(trim(_name), \'\') IS NULL OR length(trim(_name)) > 200 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_name\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF _email_normalized !~ \'^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$\'\r\n     OR length(_email_normalized) > 320 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_email\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF length(COALESCE(_notes, \'\')) > 4000 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'notes_too_long\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF length(COALESCE(_client_idempotency_key, \'\')) NOT BETWEEN 8 AND 200\r\n     OR length(COALESCE(_booking_token_hash, \'\')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256\r\n     OR length(COALESCE(_ip_hash, \'\')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_capability\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb)) <> \'array\'\r\n     OR jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb)) > 10 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_guests\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(g.email) ORDER BY g.email), \'[]\'::jsonb)\r\n  INTO _guests_normalized\r\n  FROM (\r\n    SELECT DISTINCT lower(trim(value #>> \'{}\')) AS email\r\n    FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb))\r\n    WHERE jsonb_typeof(value) = \'string\'\r\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> \'{}\')) <> _email_normalized\r\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> \'{}\')) ~ \'^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$\'\r\n  ) g;\r\n  IF jsonb_array_length(_guests_normalized) <> jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb)) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_or_duplicate_guests\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\r\n\r\n  FOR _expired IN\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      SET status = \'expired\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n          terminal_reason = \'authoritative_expiry\'\r\n    WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n      AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) <= now()\r\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, \'[)\') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, \'[)\')\r\n    RETURNING b.id, b.lifecycle_version, b.gcal_event_id\r\n  LOOP\r\n    IF _expired.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(\r\n        _expired.id, \'gcal_delete\', \'system\', \'pending\', \'expired\',\r\n        _expired.lifecycle_version, NULL\r\n      );\r\n    END IF;\r\n  END LOOP;\r\n\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\r\n        AND b.ip_hash = _ip_hash) >= 3 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'hold_cap_ip\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0429\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\r\n        AND lower(b.email) = _email_normalized) >= 3 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'hold_cap_email\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0429\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()) >= 250 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'hold_cap_global\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0429\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NOT public.booking_slot_is_clear(\r\n    _start_at, _end_at,\r\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0)\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot_conflict\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  INSERT INTO public.single_session_bookings (\r\n    start_at, end_at, name, email, notes, guests, client_idempotency_key,\r\n    draft_expires_at, user_id, booking_token_hash, booking_token_expires_at, ip_hash,\r\n    event_slug\r\n  ) VALUES (\r\n    _start_at, _end_at, trim(_name), _email_normalized, NULLIF(trim(_notes), \'\'),\r\n    _guests_normalized, _client_idempotency_key, now() + interval \'5 minutes\',\r\n    _user_id, _booking_token_hash, now() + interval \'24 hours\', _ip_hash,\r\n    _event_slug\r\n  ) RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n\r\n  RETURN _booking;\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN exclusion_violation OR unique_violation THEN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\r\n  IF FOUND THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;\r\n  RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot_conflict\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(_booking_id uuid, _expected_version integer, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\r\nRETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _can_book boolean := false;\r\n  _new_version integer;\r\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'booking_not_found\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0404\'; END IF;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\r\n  IF _event.requires_payment IS DISTINCT FROM false THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'payment_required\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status = \'booked\' THEN\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'booking_id\', _booking.id, \'status\', _booking.status,\r\n      \'lifecycle_version\', _booking.lifecycle_version, \'was_existing\', true);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status <> \'draft\' OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _expected_version THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'stale_booking\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, \'\') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, \'\') IS NULL THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'cancel_capability_required\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\r\n    _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\r\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\r\n    _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  IF NOT _can_book THEN\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n      status = \'expired\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n      terminal_reason = \'slot_taken_before_confirm\'\r\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n    IF _booking.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'gcal_delete\', \'system\', \'pending\',\r\n        \'expired\', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\r\n    END IF;\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'booking_id\', _booking.id, \'status\', _booking.status,\r\n      \'lifecycle_version\', _booking.lifecycle_version, \'was_existing\', false);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n    status = \'booked\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n    amount_cents = 0, currency = \'usd\',\r\n    cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\r\n    terminal_reason = NULL\r\n  WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n  _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'gcal_promote\', \'system\', \'pending\',\r\n    \'booked\', _new_version, NULL);\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'email_booking_confirmation\', \'booker\', \'blocked\',\r\n    \'booked\', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'email_admin_notification\', \'admin\', \'blocked\',\r\n    \'booked\', _new_version, NULL);\r\n\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'booking_id\', _booking.id, \'status\', _booking.status,\r\n    \'lifecycle_version\', _new_version, \'was_existing\', false);\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(_booking_id uuid, _provider_event_id text, _stripe_checkout_session_id text, _stripe_payment_intent_id text, _amount_cents integer, _currency text, _livemode boolean, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\r\nRETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _claimed_booking_id uuid;\r\n  _can_book boolean := false;\r\n  _new_version integer;\r\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NULLIF(_provider_event_id, \'\') IS NULL\r\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, \'\') IS NULL\r\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_payment_intent_id, \'\') IS NULL\r\n     OR _amount_cents <> 4500 OR lower(COALESCE(_currency, \'\')) <> \'usd\'\r\n     OR _livemode IS NULL THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_payment_facts\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'booking_not_found\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0404\'; END IF;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\r\n\r\n  SELECT single_session_booking_id INTO _claimed_booking_id\r\n  FROM public.booking_commerce_events\r\n  WHERE provider = \'stripe\' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id\r\n    AND event_type = \'single_session_paid\';\r\n  IF FOUND AND _claimed_booking_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking_id THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'provider_event_already_claimed\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.stripe_checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_checkout_session_id THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'checkout_mismatch\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status IN (\'booked\', \'cancelled\', \'refund_pending\', \'refunded\', \'refund_failed\') THEN\r\n    IF _booking.stripe_payment_intent_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_payment_intent_id\r\n       OR _booking.amount_cents IS DISTINCT FROM _amount_cents\r\n       OR lower(_booking.currency) IS DISTINCT FROM lower(_currency)\r\n       OR _booking.livemode IS DISTINCT FROM _livemode THEN\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION \'payment_facts_mismatch\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n    END IF;\r\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\r\n      provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\r\n      payload, single_session_booking_id\r\n    ) VALUES (\r\n      \'stripe\', _provider_event_id, \'single_session_paid\', \'single-session-native\',\r\n      _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\r\n      jsonb_build_object(\'livemode\', _livemode, \'checkout_session_id\', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\r\n      _booking_id\r\n    ) ON CONFLICT (provider, _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\r\n    AND (_booking.status = \'checkout_open\'\r\n      OR (_booking.status = \'expired\' AND _booking.status_changed_at >= now() - interval \'10 minutes\'))\r\n    AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\r\n      _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\r\n      COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\r\n      _booking.id, public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'email_admin_notification\', \'admin\', \'blocked\',\r\n      \'booked\', _new_version, NULL);\r\n  ELSE\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n      status = \'refund_pending\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\r\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _claimed_booking_id uuid;\r\n  _can_book boolean := false;\r\n  _new_version integer;\r\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NULLIF(_provider_event_id, '') IS NULL\r\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NULL\r\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_payment_intent_id, '') IS NULL\r\n SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\r\n  PERFORM [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\r\n-- Schedule events synced with Google Calendar\r\nCREATE TABLE public.schedule_events (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  google_event_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  calendar_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'primary\',\r\n  title text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'(no title)\',\r\n  description text,\r\n  location text,\r\n  start_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  end_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  all_day boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\r\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'confirmed\',\r\n  html_link text,\r\n  source text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'google\',\r\n  created_by uuid,\r\n  etag text,\r\n  last_synced_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  deleted_at timestamptz,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX idx_schedule_events_start_at ON public.schedule_events(start_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX idx_schedule_events_active ON public.schedule_events(start_at) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.schedule_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\n\r\n-- All authenticated users can read (it\'s a shared admin calendar)\r\nCREATE POLICY "Authenticated can read schedule events"\r\nON public.schedule_events FOR SELECT\r\nTO authenticated USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Only admins can write\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins can insert schedule events"\r\nON public.schedule_events FOR INSERT\r\nTO authenticated WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins can update schedule events"\r\nON public.schedule_events FOR UPDATE\r\nTO authenticated USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins can delete schedule events"\r\nON public.schedule_events FOR DELETE\r\nTO authenticated USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nCREATE TRIGGER schedule_events_updated_at\r\nBEFORE UPDATE ON public.schedule_events\r\nFOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();\r\n\r\n-- Sync state table (cursor token, selected calendar)\r\nCREATE TABLE public.gcal_sync_state (\r\n  id int PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT 1,\r\n  calendar_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'primary\',\r\n  sync_token text,\r\n  last_full_sync_at timestamptz,\r\n  last_synced_at timestamptz,\r\n  CONSTRAINT singleton CHECK (id = 1)\r\n);\r\n\r\nINSERT INTO public.gcal_sync_state (id, calendar_id) VALUES (1, \'primary\');\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.gcal_sync_state ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\n\r\nCREATE POLICY "Authenticated can read sync state"\r\nON public.gcal_sync_state FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (true);\r\n\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins can update sync state"\r\nON public.gcal_sync_state FOR UPDATE TO authenticated\r\nUSING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n-- Booking system v2, Phase 1: Google Meet URL storage, sync-retry flag,\r\n-- and DB-level double-booking guard.\r\n\r\n-- Meet URL on both the mirror and the redemption.\r\nALTER TABLE public.schedule_events\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS meet_url text;\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS meet_url text,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS needs_gcal_sync boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;\r\n\r\n-- Double-booking is enforced at the database, not by freeBusy or app-level checks.\r\n-- redeem_booking_pass locks the *pass*, not the *slot*, so two different grants\r\n-- racing the same time never contend; this exclusion constraint makes overlapping\r\n-- booked redemptions impossible regardless of interleaving.\r\nCREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist;\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS no_overlapping_bookings;\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT no_overlapping_bookings\r\n  EXCLUDE USING gist (tstzrange(start_at, end_at) WITH &&)\r\n  WHERE (status = \'booked\');\r\n-- Booking remediation Phase 3: calendar reconciliation, leases, mismatches,\r\n-- transparency, and live cron hooks.\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.schedule_events\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS transparency text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'opaque\';\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.gcal_sync_state\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS busy_calendar_ids text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::text[],\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS sync_token_updated_at timestamptz,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_attempt_at timestamptz;\r\n\r\nDO $$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint\r\n    WHERE conrelid = \'public.schedule_events\'::regclass\r\n      AND conname = \'schedule_events_transparency_ck\'\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    ALTER TABLE public.schedule_events\r\n      ADD CONSTRAINT schedule_events_transparency_ck\r\n      CHECK (transparency IN (\'opaque\', \'transparent\'));\r\n  END IF;\r\nEND $$;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_calendar_alerts (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  redemption_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_pass_redemptions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\r\n  alert_type text NOT NULL CHECK (alert_type IN (\'deleted\', \'move_conflict\', \'duplicate_event\', \'terminal_failure\', \'persistence_failure\')),\r\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'open\' CHECK (status IN (\'open\', \'resolved\', \'ignored\')),\r\n  details jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  resolved_at timestamptz,\r\n  resolved_by uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS booking_calendar_alerts_one_open\r\n  ON public.booking_calendar_alerts (redemption_id, alert_type)\r\n  WHERE status = \'open\';\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_calendar_alerts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins manage booking calendar alerts" ON public.booking_calendar_alerts;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking calendar alerts"\r\n  ON public.booking_calendar_alerts FOR ALL TO authenticated\r\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\r\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_calendar_alerts TO authenticated;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_calendar_alerts TO service_role;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_booking_calendar_actions(\r\n  _limit int DEFAULT 25,\r\n  _lease_seconds int DEFAULT 120\r\n) RETURNS SETOF public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  RETURN QUERY\r\n  WITH candidates AS (\r\n    SELECT id\r\n'}]
