[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 6.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\n\r\nimport { deleteEvent, getEvent, patchEvent, meetUrlFromEvent } from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";\r\nimport { decryptEnvelope } from "../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts";\r\nimport { createRefund, retrieveRefund } from "../_shared/stripe.ts";\r\nimport { sendAppEmail } from "../_shared/sendAppEmail.ts";\r\n\r\nconst WORKER_NAME = "single-session-outbox";\r\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 1024;\r\n\r\nfunction json(body: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction requiredEnv(name: string): string {\r\n  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\r\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function authorizeTicket(supabase: any, req: Request): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const ticket = req.headers.get("x-worker-ticket") || "";\r\n  if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(ticket)) return false;\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("consume_booking_worker_ticket", {\r\n    _token: ticket,\r\n    _worker: WORKER_NAME,\r\n  });\r\n  if (error) {\r\n    console.error("worker ticket check failed", { code: error.code });\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  return data === true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction authorize(req: Request): boolean {\r\n  const expected = Deno.env.get("BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET") ?? "";\r\n  if (!expected) return false;\r\n  const supplied = req.headers.get("x-worker-secret") || "";\r\n  if (expected.length !== supplied.length) return false;\r\n  let difference = 0;\r\n  for (let index = 0; index < expected.length; index++) difference |= expected.charCodeAt(index) ^ supplied.charCodeAt(index);\r\n  return difference === 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function heartbeat(supabase: any, phase: "start" | "success" | "error", error?: string, metadata: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\r\n  const { error: heartbeatError } = await supabase.rpc("record_booking_worker_heartbeat", {\r\n    _worker_name: WORKER_NAME,\r\n    _phase: phase,\r\n    _error: error ?? null,\r\n    _metadata: metadata,\r\n  });\r\n  if (heartbeatError) throw heartbeatError;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction whenText(startAt: string): string {\r\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {\r\n    timeZone: "America/Toronto",\r\n    weekday: "long",\r\n    month: "long",\r\n    day: "numeric",\r\n    hour: "numeric",\r\n    minute: "2-digit",\r\n    timeZoneName: "short",\r\n  }).format(new Date(startAt));\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function sendEmail(job: any, booking: any, payload: any) {\r\n  const admin = job.recipient === "admin";\r\n  const recipientEmail = admin ? requiredEnv("BOOKING_ADMIN_EMAIL") : booking.email;\r\n  if (!recipientEmail) throw new Error("email_recipient_missing");\r\n  let templateName = "booking-confirmation";\r\n  let templateData: Record<string, unknown> = {\r\n    recipientRole: admin ? "admin" : "student",\r\n    studentName: booking.name,\r\n    studentEmail: booking.email,\r\n    whenText: whenText(booking.start_at),\r\n    durationMinutes: 70,\r\n    meetUrl: booking.meet_url,\r\n    studentNote: admin ? booking.notes : "",\r\n    fileNames: [],\r\n    cancelUrl: admin ? undefined : payload?.cancel_url,\r\n    calendarPending: !booking.meet_url,\r\n  };\r\n  if (["email_cancellation", "email_refund_apology"].includes(job.action)) {\r\n    templateName = "single-session-update";\r\n    templateData = {\r\n      kind: job.action === "email_refund_apology" ? "refund" : "cancellation",\r\n      recipientRole: admin ? "admin" : "booker",\r\n      studentName: booking.name,\r\n      whenText: whenText(booking.start_at),\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n  await sendAppEmail(templateName, recipientEmail, {\r\n    idempotencyKey: job.idempotency_key,\r\n    templateData,\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function complete(supabase: any, job: any, options: { providerStatus?: string; result?: any; meetUrl?: string } = {}) {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("complete_single_session_outbox", {\r\n    _job_id: job.id,\r\n    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\r\n    _provider_status: options.providerStatus ?? null,\r\n    _result: options.result ?? {},\r\n    _meet_url: options.meetUrl ?? null,\r\n  });\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function fail(supabase: any, job: any, error: unknown, providerStatus?: string, confirmedFailure = false) {\r\n  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n  const { error: failureError } = await supabase.rpc("fail_single_session_outbox", {\r\n    _job_id: job.id,\r\n    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\r\n    _error: message,\r\n    _provider_status: providerStatus ?? null,\r\n    _confirmed_failure: confirmedFailure,\r\n  });\r\n  if (failureError && failureError.code !== "P0409") throw failureError;\r\n  return message;\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);\r\n  try {\r\n    const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");\r\n    if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);\r\n    const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\r\n    // Two accepted callers: a worker holding BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET, and the\r\n    // scheduled job, which presents a single-use ticket only a service-role\r\n    // reader could have minted. No shared secret lives in SQL or cron metadata.\r\n    if (!authorize(req) && !(await authorizeTicket(supabase, req))) {\r\n      return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);\r\n    }\r\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "start");\r\n    const workerId = `${WORKER_NAME}:${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\r\n    const { data: jobs, error: claimError } = await supabase.rpc("claim_single_session_outbox", {\r\n      _worker_id: workerId,\r\n      _actions: null,\r\n      _limit: 20,\r\n      _lease_seconds: 180,\r\n    });\r\n    if (claimError) throw claimError;\r\n\r\n    const results: { id: string; action: string; outcome: string }[] = [];\r\n    for (const job of jobs ?? []) {\r\n      const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n        .select("*").eq("id", job.booking_id).maybeSingle();\r\n      if (bookingError || !booking) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, bookingError ?? new Error("booking_not_found"));\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n      if (booking.status !== job.precondition_status || booking.lifecycle_version !== job.precondition_lifecycle_version) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, new Error("lifecycle_precondition_changed"));\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "superseded" });\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      try {\r\n        if (job.action === "gcal_promote") {\r\n          if (!booking.gcal_event_id) throw new Error("tentative_event_missing");\r\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\r\n          const targetCalendar = state?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n          const attendees = [booking.email, ...((booking.guests ?? []) as string[])].filter(Boolean).map((email) => ({ email }));\r\n          let event = await patchEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id, {\r\n            summary: `${String(booking.name || "Student").trim().split(/\\s+/)[0]} session`,\r\n            description: booking.notes ? `Booked via LSAT U.\\n\\nNotes from Booker:\\n${booking.notes}` : "Booked via LSAT U.",\r\n            attendees,\r\n            visibility: "default",\r\n            conferenceData: { createRequest: { requestId: booking.id, conferenceSolutionKey: { type: "hangoutsMeet" } } },\r\n            extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_single_session_booking_id: booking.id, lsatu_hold: "false" } },\r\n          }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: "all" });\r\n          let meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\r\n          if (!meetUrl) {\r\n            event = await getEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id);\r\n            meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\r\n          }\r\n          if (!meetUrl) throw new Error("meet_url_pending");\r\n          const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").upsert({\r\n            google_event_id: event.id,\r\n            calendar_id: targetCalendar,\r\n            title: event.summary,\r\n            description: event.description,\r\n            start_at: booking.start_at,\r\n            end_at: booking.end_at,\r\n            all_day: false,\r\n            status: event.status ?? "confirmed",\r\n            html_link: event.htmlLink ?? null,\r\n            meet_url: meetUrl,\r\n            source: "single_session",\r\n            etag: event.etag ?? null,\r\n            last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n            deleted_at: null,\r\n          }, { onConflict: "google_event_id" });\r\n          if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\r\n          const completion = await complete(supabase, job, { meetUrl, result: { google_event_id: event.id } });\r\n          if (completion?.compensate) {\r\n            await deleteEvent(targetCalendar, event.id, { sendUpdates: "all" });\r\n            await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({ status: "cancelled", deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("google_event_id", event.id);\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (job.action === "gcal_delete") {\r\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\r\n          const eventId = booking.gcal_event_id;\r\n          if (eventId) {\r\n            await deleteEvent(state?.calendar_id || "primary", eventId, { sendUpdates: booking.status === "cancelled" ? "all" : "none" });\r\n            const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({\r\n              status: "cancelled",\r\n              deleted_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n              last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n            }).eq("google_event_id", eventId);\r\n            if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\r\n          }\r\n          await complete(supabase, job, { result: { google_event_id: eventId ?? null } });\r\n        } else if (job.action === "stripe_refund") {\r\n          if (!booking.stripe_payment_intent_id) throw new Error("payment_intent_missing");\r\n          const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\r\n          const refund = booking.stripe_refund_id\r\n            ? await retrieveRefund(stripeSecret, booking.stripe_refund_id)\r\n            : await createRefund(stripeSecret, {\r\n              payment_intent: booking.stripe_payment_intent_id,\r\n              amount: 4500,\r\n              metadata: { booking_id: booking.id, product: "single_session" },\r\n            }, job.idempotency_key);\r\n          if (refund.status === "succeeded") {\r\n            await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: refund.status, result: { refund_id: refund.id } });\r\n          } else if (["failed", "canceled"].includes(String(refund.status))) {\r\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_confirmed_failed"), String(refund.status), true);\r\n          } else {\r\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_pending"), String(refund.status), false);\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (job.action.startsWith("email_")) {\r\n          const payload = job.payload_ciphertext\r\n            ? await decryptEnvelope(job.payload_ciphertext, requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY"))\r\n            : {};\r\n          await sendEmail(job, booking, payload);\r\n          await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: "accepted" });\r\n        } else {\r\n          throw new Error("unknown_outbox_action");\r\n        }\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "processed" });\r\n      } catch (error) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, error);\r\n        console.error("single-session outbox job failed", { bookingId: booking.id, jobId: job.id, action: job.action, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "success", undefined, { processed: results.length });\r\n    return json({ processed: results.length, results });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n    try {\r\n      const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\r\n      await heartbeat(supabase, "error", message);\r\n    } catch (_) { /* fail-closed response is the durable alert when heartbeat is unavailable */ }\r\n    console.error("single-session-outbox error", { error: message });\r\n    return json({ error: "worker_failed" }, 500);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n'}]

_student_id AND s.enabled\r\n      AND (o.active OR (o.id = 'single-session'\r\n        AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), '') IS NOT NULL\r\n        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE '/%'))\r\n  )\r\n  SELECT * FROM default_offers\r\n  UNION ALL\r\n  SELECT * FROM arranged_offers;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_passes\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT booking_passes_reserved_session_slug_chk\r\n  CHECK (booking_slug IS NULL OR lower(booking_slug) <> 'session') NOT VALID;\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_passes VALIDATE CONSTRAINT booking_passes_reserved_session_slug_chk;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_outbox(\r\n  _worker_id text,\r\n  _actions text[] DEFAULT NULL,\r\n  _limit integer DEFAULT 20,\r\n  _lease_seconds integer DEFAULT 120\r\n) RETURNS SETOF public.single_session_outbox\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NULLIF(trim(_worker_id), '') IS NULL OR _limit NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 100\r\n     OR _lease_seconds NOT BETWEEN 10 AND 900 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_lease_request' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_outbox o SET\r\n    state = 'superseded', lease_worker_id = NULL, lease_token = NULL,\r\n    lease_until = NULL, updated_at = now(),\r\n    last_error = COALESCE(o.last_error, 'lifecycle precondition no longer holds')\r\n  FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n  WHERE b.id = o.booking_id\r\n    AND o.state IN ('pending', 'failed', 'leased')\r\n    AND (o.state <> 'leased' OR o.lease_until <= now())\r\n    AND (b.status <> o.precondition_status\r\n      OR b.lifecycle_version <> o.precondition_lifecycle_version);\r\n\r\n  RETURN QUERY\r\n  WITH candidates AS (\r\n    SELECT o.id\r\n    FROM public.single_session_outbox o\r\n    WHERE (o.state = 'pending'\r\n        OR (o.state = 'leased' AND o.lease_until <= now()))\r\n      AND o.next_retry_at <= now()\r\n      AND o.attempts < o.max_attempts\r\n      AND (_actions IS NULL OR o.action = ANY(_actions))\r\n    ORDER BY o.next_retry_at, o.created_at\r\n    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED\r\n    LIMIT _limit\r\n  )\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_outbox o SET\r\n    state = 'leased', attempts = o.attempts + 1,\r\n    lease_worker_id = _worker_id, lease_token = gen_random_uuid(),\r\n    lease_until = now() + make_interval(secs => _lease_seconds), updated_at = now()\r\n  FROM candidates c WHERE o.id = c.id\r\n  RETURNING o.*;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.complete_single_session_outbox(\r\n  _job_id uuid,\r\n  _lease_token uuid,\r\n  _provider_status text DEFAULT NULL,\r\n  _result jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\r\n  _meet_url text DEFAULT NULL\r\n) RETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _job public.single_session_outbox%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _compensate boolean := false;\r\n  _refund_id text;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _job FROM public.single_session_outbox WHERE id = _job_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox_job_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\r\n  IF _job.state <> 'leased' OR _job.lease_token IS DISTINCT FROM _lease_token\r\n     OR _job.lease_until <= now() THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox_lease_lost' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('single-session-side-effect:' || _job.booking_id::text, 0));\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _job.booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n  IF _booking.status <> _job.precondition_status\r\n     OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _job.precondition_lifecycle_version THEN\r\n    _compensate := _job.action = 'gcal_promote';\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET\r\n      state = 'superseded', lease_worker_id = NULL, lease_token = NULL,\r\n      lease_until = NULL, last_error = 'lifecycle changed during side effect', updated_at = now()\r\n    WHERE id = _job_id;\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('applied', false, 'compensate', _compensate);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _job.action = 'gcal_promote' THEN\r\n    IF NULLIF(_meet_url, '') IS NULL THEN\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'meet_url_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n    END IF;\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings SET meet_url = _meet_url WHERE id = _booking.id;\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET state = 'pending', next_retry_at = now(), updated_at = now()\r\n    WHERE booking_id = _booking.id AND state = 'blocked'\r\n      AND action IN ('email_booking_confirmation', 'email_admin_notification')\r\n      AND precondition_status = _booking.status\r\n      AND precondition_lifecycle_version = _booking.lifecycle_version;\r\n  ELSIF _job.action = 'stripe_refund' THEN\r\n    _refund_id := NULLIF(_result->>'refund_id', '');\r\n    IF _refund_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.single_session_bookings SET\r\n        stripe_refund_id = COALESCE(stripe_refund_id, _refund_id),\r\n        stripe_refund_status = COALESCE(_provider_status, stripe_refund_status)\r\n      WHERE id = _booking.id;\r\n    END IF;\r\n    IF lower(COALESCE(_provider_status, '')) = 'succeeded' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n        status = 'refunded', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n        stripe_refund_status = 'succeeded'\r\n      WHERE b.id = _booking.id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_refund_apology', 'booker', 'pending',\r\n        'refunded', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\r\n    END IF;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET\r\n    state = 'succeeded', provider_status = _provider_status,\r\n    result = COALESCE(_result, '{}'::jsonb), payload_ciphertext = NULL,\r\n    sensitive_payload_purged_at = CASE WHEN payload_ciphertext IS NULL\r\n      THEN sensitive_payload_purged_at ELSE now() END,\r\n    lease_worker_id = NULL, lease_token = NULL, lease_until = NULL,\r\n    completed_at = now(), updated_at = now()\r\n  WHERE id = _job_id;\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('applied', true, 'compensate', false);\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fail_single_session_outbox(\r\n  _job_id uuid,\r\n  _lease_token uuid,\r\n  _error text,\r\n  _provider_status text DEFAULT NULL,\r\n  _confirmed_failure boolean DEFAULT false\r\n) RETURNS public.single_session_outbox\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _job public.single_session_outbox%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _terminal boolean;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _job FROM public.single_session_outbox WHERE id = _job_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox_job_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\r\n  IF _job.state <> 'leased' OR _job.lease_token IS DISTINCT FROM _lease_token THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox_lease_lost' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _job.booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status <> _job.precondition_status\r\n     OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _job.precondition_lifecycle_version THEN\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET\r\n      state = 'superseded', lease_worker_id = NULL, lease_token = NULL,\r\n      lease_until = NULL, last_error = 'lifecycle changed during side effect', updated_at = now()\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\nimport { corsHeaders } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2/cors";\r\n\r\nimport {\r\n  deleteEvent,\r\n  freeBusy,\r\n  getEvent,\r\n  insertEvent,\r\n  listEvents,\r\n  meetUrlFromEvent,\r\n  patchEvent,\r\n} from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";\r\nimport {\r\n  loadBusyIntervals,\r\n  parseAvailability,\r\n  type GoogleCal,\r\n} from "../_shared/bookingCore.ts";\r\nimport {\r\n  buildCheckoutFingerprint,\r\n  deterministicSingleSessionEventId,\r\n  generateSingleSessionSlots,\r\n  hasConflictingCalendarEvent,\r\n  hostLocalToUtc,\r\n  normalizeEmail,\r\n  normalizeGuests,\r\n  SINGLE_SESSION,\r\n  SINGLE_SESSION_EVENT,\r\n  singleSessionWindowEndDate,\r\n  toEventConfig,\r\n  toPublicBooking,\r\n  type BookingEventConfig,\r\n} from "../_shared/singleSessionCore.ts";\r\nimport {\r\n  decryptEnvelope,\r\n  encryptEnvelope,\r\n  generateCapabilityToken,\r\n  hmacHex,\r\n  sha256Hex,\r\n} from "../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts";\r\nimport { createCheckoutSession, expireCheckoutSession } from "../_shared/stripe.ts";\r\nimport { userFromRequest } from "../_shared/authClaims.ts";\r\n\r\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 8192;\r\nconst READ_LIMIT = 30;\r\nconst WRITE_LIMIT = 5;\r\nconst APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get("APP_ORIGIN") || "https://lsatprep.study";\r\nconst slotsCache = new Map<string, { expiresAt: number; payload: { slots: unknown[]; timezone: string } }>();\r\n\r\nfunction slotUnavailable(eventSlug: string) {\r\n  slotsCache.delete(eventSlug);\r\n  return json({ error: "slot_unavailable" }, 409);\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst gcal: GoogleCal = { insertEvent, patchEvent, getEvent, deleteEvent, freeBusy, meetUrlFromEvent };\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The capability token the caller gets back for a free booking. Only the caller\r\n * that minted this token may cancel with it, so a replay from a different\r\n * request gets nothing rather than someone else\'s token.\r\n */\r\nfunction responseBookingTokenForFree(\r\n  booking: { booking_token_hash?: string | null },\r\n  bookingTokenHash: string,\r\n  bookingToken: string,\r\n): string {\r\n  return booking.booking_token_hash === bookingTokenHash ? bookingToken : "";\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Is the host\'s calendar actually free for this booking\'s window, ignoring the\r\n * booking\'s own tentative event. Mirrors the check the Stripe webhook runs\r\n * before it promotes a paid hold.\r\n */\r\nasync function calendarClearFor(supabase: any, booking: any): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const { data: state, error } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")\r\n    .select("calendar_id,busy_calendar_ids").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  const ids = [...new Set([state?.calendar_id || "primary", ...(state?.busy_calendar_ids ?? [])])];\r\n  for (const calendar of ids) {\r\n    const page = await listEvents(calendar, {\r\n      timeMin: booking.start_at,\r\n      timeMax: booking.end_at,\r\n      singleEvents: "true",\r\n      showDeleted: "false",\r\n      maxResults: "50",\r\n    });\r\n    if (hasConflictingCalendarEvent(page?.items ?? [], booking.start_at, booking.end_at, booking.gcal_event_id)) {\r\n      return false;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  return true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction json(body: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\r\n    status,\r\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction requiredEnv(name: string): string {\r\n  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\r\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function readBody(req: Request): Promise<any> {\r\n  const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");\r\n  if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");\r\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await req.arrayBuffer());\r\n  if (bytes.byteLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");\r\n  return bytes.byteLength ? JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) : {};\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction clientIp(req: Request): string {\r\n  const platformIp = req.headers.get("cf-connecting-ip") || req.headers.get("fly-client-ip") || req.headers.get("x-real-ip");\r\n  if (platformIp?.trim()) return platformIp.trim();\r\n  const hops = (req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || "").split(",").map((part) => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);\r\n  return hops.at(-1) || "unknown";\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function verifyTurnstile(token: string, ip: string): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const secret = requiredEnv("TURNSTILE_SECRET");\r\n  if (!token) return false;\r\n  const response = await fetch("https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify", {\r\n    method: "POST",\r\n    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },\r\n    body: new URLSearchParams({ secret, response: token, remoteip: ip }),\r\n  });\r\n  if (!response.ok) return false;\r\n  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\r\n  return result?.success === true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function rateLimit(supabase: any, ipHash: string, action: string, limit: number): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("bump_booking_hit", {\r\n    _ip_hash: ipHash,\r\n    _slug: "single-session",\r\n    _action: action,\r\n  });\r\n  // Fail open. "",\r\n    );\r\n    const caller = await userFromRequest(req);\r\n    if (!caller) return false;\r\n    const { data: role } = await admin\r\n      .from("user_roles")\r\n      .select("role")\r\n      .eq("user_id", caller.id)\r\n      .eq("role", "admin")\r\n      .maybeSingle();\r\n    return !!role;\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function optionalUserId(supabase: any, req: Request): Promise<string | null> {\r\n  const authorization = [REDACTED] || "";\r\n  if (!authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) return null;\r\n  const caller = await userFromRequest(req);\r\n  return caller?.id ?? null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction checkoutDescription(startAt: string, timezone: string): string {\r\n  let tz = timezone || SINGLE_SESSION.hostTimezone;\r\n  try { new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: tz }).format(); }\r\n  catch (_) { tz = SINGLE_SESSION.hostTimezone; }\r\n  const formatted = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {\r\n    timeZone: tz,\r\n    weekday: "long",\r\n    month: "long",\r\n    day: "numeric",\r\n    hour: "numeric",\r\n    minute: "2-digit",\r\n  }).format(new Date(startAt));\r\n  return `LSAT Session with Joshua — ${formatted} (${tz})`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction bookingTokenFromUrl(value: string): string | null {\r\n  try { return new URL(value).searchParams.get("booking_token"); }\r\n  catch (_) { return null; }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function calendarId(supabase: any): Promise<string> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function adoptTentativeEvent(calendar: string, bookingId: string, startAt: string, endAt: string): Promise<any> {\r\n  const eventId = deterministicSingleSessionEventId(bookingId);\r\n  let event: any = null;\r\n  try {\r\n    event = await insertEvent(calendar, {\r\n      id: eventId,\r\n      summary: "Reserved LSAT session",\r\n      description: "Temporary booking hold.",\r\n      start: { dateTime: startAt },\r\n      end: { dateTime: endAt },\r\n      transparency: "opaque",\r\n      visibility: "private",\r\n      extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_single_session_booking_id: bookingId, lsatu_hold: "true" } },\r\n    }, { sendUpdates: "none" });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    try { event = await getEvent(calendar, eventId); }\r\n    catch (_) {\r\n      const found = await listEvents(calendar, {\r\n        privateExtendedProperty: `lsatu_single_session_booking_id=${bookingId}`,\r\n        showDeleted: "false",\r\n        maxResults: "2",\r\n      });\r\n      event = found?.items?.find((item: any) => item.status !== "cancelled") ?? null;\r\n    }\r\n    if (!event) throw error;\r\n  }\r\n  return event;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function cleanupTentative(calendar: string, bookingId: string, knownEventId?: string | null) {\r\n  const eventId = knownEventId || deterministicSingleSessionEventId(bookingId);\r\n  try { await deleteEvent(calendar, eventId, { sendUpdates: "none" }); }\r\n  catch (error) { console.error("single-session calendar cleanup failed", { bookingId, eventId, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) }); }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The event this request is about. Defaults to the single session so an older\r\n * client that sends no slug keeps working exactly as it did.\r\n */\r\nasync function loadEvent(supabase: any, slug: unknown): Promise<BookingEventConfig> {\r\n  const wanted = String(slug || "single-session").trim().slice(0, 64) || "single-session";\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from("booking_event_types")\r\n    .select("*")\r\n    .eq("slug", wanted)\r\n    .eq("active", true)\r\n    .maybeSingle();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  if (!data) {\r\n    // An unknown slug must not silently fall back to the paid session.\r\n    if (wanted !== "single-session") throw new Error("unknown_event");\r\n    return SINGLE_SESSION_EVENT;\r\n  }\r\n  return toEventConfig(data);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Has this one-off link already been used. Ordinary events are never spent, so\r\n * this is a single indexed lookup that answers false for every existing caller.\r\n */\r\nasync function linkSpent(supabase: any, event: BookingEventConfig): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  if (!event.singleUse) return false;\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("booking_event_is_spent", { _event_slug: event.slug });\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data === true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\r\n  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    const body = await readBody(req);\r\n    const action = String(body.action || "");\r\n    const supabaseUrl = requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL");\r\n    const serviceKey = requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");\r\n    const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey);\r\n    const ip = clientIp(req);\r\n    // Keyed when the secret is there, plain digest when it is not. Either way\r\n    // it is only a bucket key for counting hits, and a missing secret must not\r\n    // cost every student the ability to book.\r\n    const hmacKey = Deno.env.get("BOOKING_IP_HMAC_KEY");\r\n    if (!hmacKey) console.error("BOOKING_IP_HMAC_KEY is not set; throttle keys are unkeyed digests");\r\n    const ipHash = hmacKey ? await hmacHex(hmacKey, ip) : await sha256Hex(ip);\r\n    const isWrite = ["hold", "abandon", "cancel"].includes(action);\r\n    if (!await rateLimit(supabase, ipHash, isWrite ? action : "read", isWrite ? blocks for the whole window plus the buffers on both ends, so\r\n      // an event just outside the window still blocks the slot next to it.\r\n      const bufferMs = payload });\r\n      return json(payload);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "status") {\r\n      const checkoutSessionId = String(body.checkout_session_id || "");\r\n      const token = return json({ error: "invalid_token" }, error?.code === "P0403" ? 403 : 404);\r\n      return json(data);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "hold") {\r\n      const name = String(body.name || New claims still take the live observation immediately before\r\n      // the authoritative SQL claim.\r\n      const { data: existingReplay, error: replayLookupError } = await supabase\r\n        .from("single_session_bookings")\r\n        .select("id")\r\n        .eq("client_idempotency_key", clientIdempotencyKey)\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n      if (replayLookupError) throw replayLookupError;\r\n      if (!existingReplay) {\r\n        const liveBusy = await loadBusyIntervals({\r\n          supabase,\r\n          gcal,\r\n          rangeStartIso: startAt.toISOString(),\r\n          rangeEndIso: endAt.toISOString(),\r\n        });\r\n        if (liveBusy.some((interval) => interval.start < endAt.getTime() && interval.end > startAt.getTime())) {\r\n          return slotUnavailable(event.slug);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const replayToken = String(body.booking_token || "");\r\n      const bookingToken = replayToken || generateCapabilityToken();\r\n      const bookingTokenHash = await sha256Hex(bookingToken);\r\n      const { data: claimed, error: claimError } = await supabase.rpc("claim_single_session_slot", {\r\n        _start_at: startAt.toISOString(),\r\n        _name: name,\r\n        _email: email,\r\n        _notes: notes || null,\r\n        _guests: guests,\r\n        _client_idempotency_key: clientIdempotencyKey,\r\n        _booking_token_hash: bookingTokenHash,\r\n        _ip_hash: ipHash,\r\n        _user_id: await optionalUserId(supabase, req),\r\n        _event_slug: event.slug,\r\n      });\r\n      if (claimError) {\r\n        // P0410 is a one-off link that was already used. "rate_limited" : status === 400 ? "invalid_request" : "server_error" }, status);\r\n      }\r\n      let booking = Array.isArray(claimed) ? claimed[0] : claimed;\r\n      if (!booking) throw new Error("claim_returned_no_booking");\r\n      const encryptionSecret = requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY");\r\n      if (booking.status !== "draft") {\r\n        let responseBookingToken = booking.booking_token_hash === bookingTokenHash ? null,\r\n          checkout_url: booking.stripe_checkout_url ?? null,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const targetCalendar = await calendarId(supabase);\r\n      let tentative: any;\r\n      try {\r\n        tentative = await adoptTentativeEvent(targetCalendar, booking.id, booking.start_at, booking.end_at);\r\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("set_single_session_tentative_event", {\r\n          _booking_id: booking.id,\r\n          _expected_version: booking.lifecycle_version,\r\n          _gcal_event_id: tentative.id,\r\n        });\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        booking = data ?? booking;\r\n      } catch (calendarError) {\r\n        await supabase.rpc("expire_single_session_draft", {\r\n          _booking_id: booking.id,\r\n          _expected_version: booking.lifecycle_version,\r\n          _reason: "tentative_calendar_failed",\r\n        });\r\n        await cleanupTentative(targetCalendar, booking.id, tentative?.id);\r\n        console.error("single-session tentative event failed", { bookingId: booking.id, error: calendarError instanceof Error ? calendarError.message : String(calendarError) });\r\n        return slotUnavailable(event.slug);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // A free event has no middle step. The worker secret is\r\n// read from the function environment and never logged or returned. Failures are\r\n// swallowed: the every-minute scheduled worker is the retry safety net, and a\r\n// paid booking must never be undone by a missed nudge.\r\nasync function kickOutboxWorker(): Promise<void> {\r\n  const secret = Deno.env.get("BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET");\r\n  if (!secret) {\r\n    console.error("outbox nudge skipped: worker secret not configured");\r\n    return;\r\n  }\r\n  try {\r\n    const res = await fetch(`${requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL")}/functions/v1/single-session-outbox`, {\r\n      method: "POST",\r\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "x-worker-secret": secret },\r\n      body: "{}",\r\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),\r\n    });\r\n    if (!res.ok) console.error("outbox nudge non-2xx", { status: res.status });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    console.error("outbox nudge failed", { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function handleStripe(supabase: any, raw: string, signature: string | null) {\r\n  const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\r\n  if (!signature) return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);\r\n  const stripe = new Stripe(stripeSecret, {\r\n    apiVersion: "2025-06-30.basil",\r\n    httpClient: Stripe.createFetchHttpClient(),\r\n  });\r\n  let event: Stripe.Event;\r\n  try {\r\n    event = await stripe.webhooks.constructEventAsync(\r\n      raw,\r\n      signature,\r\n      requiredEnv("STRIPE_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET"),\r\n      300,\r\n    );\r\n  } catch (_) {\r\n    return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (event.type.startsWith("refund.")) {\r\n    const refund = event.data.object;\r\n    if (event.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n    const bookingId = String(refund.metadata?.booking_id || "");\r\n    if (!bookingId || !refund.id || !refund.status) return json({ error: "invalid_refund" }, 400);\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("record_single_session_refund_status", {\r\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\r\n      _stripe_refund_id: refund.id,\r\n      _provider_status: refund.status,\r\n    });\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    return json({ received: true });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (event.type === "checkout.session.expired") {\r\n    const session = event.data.object;\r\n    if (session.metadata?.product !== "single_session") return json({ received: true, ignored: "not_single_session" });\r\n    if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");\r\n    if (!bookingId || !session.id) return json({ error: "invalid_session" }, 400);\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("expire_single_session_checkout", {\r\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\r\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\r\n      _reason: "stripe_checkout_expired",\r\n    });\r\n    if (error && error.code !== "P0409") throw error;\r\n    return json({ received: true });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (event.type !== "checkout.session.completed") return json({ received: true });\r\n  const session = event.data.object;\r\n\r\n  // Any completed payment opens the product for that email, whatever it was\r\n  // for. A single session, a package, a bootcamp: someone who has paid Joshua\r\n  // money is a student, and the branches below still do their own work.\r\n  if (session.payment_status === "paid") {\r\n    try {\r\n      const grant = await grantStudentAccess(\r\n        supabase,\r\n        session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null,\r\n      );\r\n      console.log("access grant", { event: event.id, ...grant });\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      // Access is a convenience on top of the payment record. Failing to open\r\n      // it must never make Stripe retry a payment that was already recorded.\r\n      console.error("access grant failed", { event: event.id, err });\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (session.metadata?.product === "single_session") {\r\n    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");\r\n    if (!bookingId) return json({ error: "invalid_product" }, 400);\r\n    if (!session.id || typeof session.payment_intent !== "string") return json({ error: "missing_payment_facts" }, 400);\r\n    const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n      .select("id,status,start_at,end_at,gcal_event_id,event_slug")\r\n      .eq("id", bookingId).maybeSingle();\r\n    if (bookingError) throw bookingError;\r\n    if (!booking) return json({ error: "unknown_booking" }, 400);\r\n    const { data: bookingEvent, error: eventError } = await supabase.from("booking_event_types")\r\n      .select("price_cents,currency")\r\n      .eq("slug", booking.event_slug).maybeSingle();\r\n    if (eventError) throw eventError;\r\n    if (!bookingEvent) return json({ error: "unknown_event" }, 400);\r\n    const validation = validateSingleSessionCheckout(\r\n      session,\r\n      expectedLivemode(),\r\n      Number(bookingEvent.price_cents),\r\n      String(bookingEvent.currency),\r\n    );\r\n    if (!validation.ok) return json({ error: validation.error }, 400);\r\n\r\n    const cancelToken = generateCapabilityToken();\r\n    const encryptedCancelPayload = await encryptEnvelope({\r\n { data: result, error } = await supabase.rpc("finalize_single_session_payment", {\r\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\r\n      _provider_event_id: event.id,\r\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\r\n      _stripe_payment_intent_id: session.payment_intent,\r\n      _amount_cents: session.amount_total,\r\n      _currency: session.currency,\r\n      _livemode: session.livemode,\r\n      _live_calendar_clear: await liveCalendarClear(supabase, booking),\r\n      _cancel_token_hash: await sha256Hex(cancelToken),\r\n      _cancel_payload_ciphertext: encryptedCancelPayload,\r\n    });\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    // Keep the Stripe response fast: the nudge runs after the response is sent\r\n    // when the runtime supports it, and is otherwise best-effort.\r\n    const nudge = kickOutboxWorker();\r\n    const waitUntil = (globalThis as any)?.EdgeRuntime?.waitUntil;\r\n    if (typeof waitUntil === "function") waitUntil.call((globalThis as any).EdgeRuntime, nudge);\r\n    else void nudge;\r\n    return json({ received: true, booking_id: result?.booking_id, status: result?.status });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Existing package branch, now protected by immutable payment validation.\r\n  if (session.mode !== "payment" || session.payment_status !== "paid") return json({ error: "payment_not_complete" }, 400);\r\n  if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) (String(session.currency || "").toLowerCase() !== expected.currency) return json({ error: "currency_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n  const email = session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null;\r\n  const student = await matchStudent(supabase, email, null);\r\n  if (!student) {\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.from("booking_commerce_events").upsert({\r\n      provider: "stripe",\r\n      provider_event_id: event.id,\r\n      event_type: "unmatched_paid",\r\n      offer_id: offer.id,\r\n      email,\r\n      amount_cents: session.amount_total,\r\n      currency: session.currency,\r\n      payload: { checkout_session_id: session.id, payment_link: paymentLink, payment_status: session.payment_status, livemode: session.livemode },\r\n    }, { onConflict: The shared host-day advisory\n# lock plus the occupying exclusion constraint must let exactly one commit.\nrace_start="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT race_start FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\nrace_sql_a="SELECT id FROM public.claim_single_session_slot(\'$race_start\'::timestamptz,\'Race A\',\'race-a@example.com\',NULL,\'[]\'::jsonb,\'idem-real-race-a\',repeat(\'1\',32),repeat(\'2\',32),NULL);"\nrace_sql_b="SELECT id FROM public.claim_single_session_slot(\'$race_start\'::timestamptz,\'Race B\',\'race-b@example.com\',NULL,\'[]\'::jsonb,\'idem-real-race-b\',repeat(\'3\',32),repeat(\'4\',32),NULL);"\nset +e\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$race_sql_a" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\nrace_pid_a=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$race_sql_b" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\nrace_pid_b=$!\nwait "$race_pid_a"\nrace_status_a=$?\nwait "$race_pid_b"\nrace_status_b=$?\nset -e\nif { test "$race_status_a" -eq 0 && test "$race_status_b" -eq 0; } || \\\n   { test "$race_status_a" -ne 0 && test "$race_status_b" -ne 0; }; then\n  echo "native claim race did not produce exactly one winner" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\nrace_winners="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE client_idempotency_key IN (\'idem-real-race-a\',\'idem-real-race-b\')")"\ntest "$race_winners" = "1"\n\n# Cross-engine race: a package redemption and a native Hold contend for the\n# same clean slot. Both paths must use the same host-day advisory lock.\ncross_race_start="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT cross_race_start FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\ncross_race_pass="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT cross_race_pass_id FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\ncross_native_sql="SELECT id FROM public.claim_single_session_slot(\'$cross_race_start\'::timestamptz,\'Cross Native\',\'cross-native@example.com\',NULL,\'[]\'::jsonb,\'idem-real-cross-native\',repeat(\'5\',32),repeat(\'6\',32),NULL);"\ncross_package_sql="SELECT redemption_id FROM public.redeem_booking_pass_v2(\'$cross_race_pass\'::uuid,\'idem-real-cross-package\',\'$cross_race_start\'::timestamptz,NULL,\'Cross Package\',\'cross-package@example.com\');"\nset +e\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cross_native_sql" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\ncross_pid_native=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cross_package_sql" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\ncross_pid_package=$!\nwait "$cross_pid_native"\ncross_status_native=$?\nwait "$cross_pid_package"\ncross_status_package=$?\nset -e\nif { test "$cross_status_native" -eq 0 && test "$cross_status_package" -eq 0; } || \\\n   { test "$cross_status_native" -ne 0 && test "$cross_status_package" -ne 0; }; then\n  echo "cross-engine claim race did not produce exactly one winner" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\ncross_native_count="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE client_idempotency_key=\'idem-real-cross-native\'")"\ncross_package_count="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions WHERE booking_pass_id=\'$cross_race_pass\' AND idempotency_key=\'idem-real-cross-package\'")"\ncross_used="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT used_credits FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id=\'$cross_race_pass\'")"\ntest $((cross_native_count + cross_package_count)) -eq 1\ntest "$cross_used" = "$cross_package_count"\n\n"${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/tests/booking/phase_concurrency.sql" >/dev/null\n\ncancel_sql="SELECT public.cancel_booking_redemption(\'20000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\', true, NULL);"\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cancel_sql" >/dev/null &\nfirst_pid=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cancel_sql" >/dev/null &\nsecond_pid=$!\nwait "$first_pid"\nwait "$second_pid"\n\nresult="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT used_credits || \':\' || status FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id=\'10000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\'")"\nrefund="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT credit_refunded::text || \':\' || status || \':\' || calendar_action FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions WHERE id=\'20000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\'")"\ntest "$result" = "0:active"\ntest "$refund" = "true:cancelled:delete"\n\necho "booking database migrations and double-cancel concurrency: PASS"\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '277:export function toPublicBooking(row: any) {\nimport { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";\r\nimport {\r\n  CalendarDays, Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Clock, ExternalLink,\r\n  Loader2, Plus, ShieldCheck, Trash2, Video,\r\n} from "lucide-react";\r\nimport { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";\r\nimport { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";\r\nimport { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";\r\nimport { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";\r\nimport { cn } from "@/lib/utils";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport "@/pages/booking/booking-theme.css";\r\nimport { buildMonthCells, dayKey, groupSlotsByDay, slotDays, startOfDay } from "@/components/booking/bookingCalendar";\r\n\r\nexport type SingleSessionSlot = { start_at: string; end_at: string };\r\nexport type SingleSessionStatus = {\r\n  status: "draft" | "checkout_open" | "booked" | "cancelled" | "expired" | "abandoned" | "refund_pending" | "refunded" | "refund_failed";\r\n  start_at: string;\r\n  end_at: string;\r\n  meet_url?: string | null;\r\n  checkout_url?: string | null;\r\n};\r\ntype HoldInput = {\r\n  start_at: string;\r\n  name: string;\r\n  email: string;\r\n  notes: string;\r\n  guests: string[];\r\n  timezone: string;\r\n  client_idempotency_key: string;\r\n  turnstile_token: string;\r\n  booking_token?: string;\r\n};\r\n// A free event finishes inside `hold`: there is no checkout to send anyone to,\r\n// so the url and session id come back null and the booking is already booked.\r\ntype HoldResult = {\r\n  checkout_url: string | null;\r\n  booking_token: string;\r\n  checkout_session_id: string | null;\r\n  booking?: SingleSessionStatus;\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport type EventInfo = {\r\n  host: { name: string; title: string };\r\n  duration_minutes: number;\r\n  amount_cents: number;\r\n  currency: string;\r\n  requires_payment?: boolean;\r\n  /** True for a one-off link: this URL sells one session and then stops. */\r\n  single_use?: boolean;\r\n  /** False once a one-off link has been used. Undefined for ordinary events. */\r\n  available?: boolean;\r\n};\r\n\r\n/** "1h 10m", "30m" -- the event\'s own length, not a constant. */\r\nfunction durationLabel(minutes: number): string {\r\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\r\n  const rest = minutes % 60;\r\n  if (!hours) return `${rest}m`;\r\n  return rest ? `${hours}h ${rest}m` : `${hours}h`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** The wire words the edge function uses, as something a person can read. */\r\nconst ERROR_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {\r\n  unknown_event: "This booking link is no longer active.",\r\n  link_already_used: "This link has already been used to book a session.",\r\n  slot_unavailable: "That time was taken while you were filling this in. Please pick another.",\r\n  rate_limited: "Too many attempts. Please wait a moment and try again.",\r\n  security_check_failed: "The security check did not pass. Please reload and try again.",\r\n  security_check_unavailable: "The security check could not load. Please reload and try again.",\r\n  security_check_expired: "The security check expired. Please try again.",\r\n  verification_failed: "The security check did not pass. Please reload and try again.",\r\n};\r\n\r\nfunction readableError(message: string): string {\r\n  return ERROR_TEXT[message] ?? message;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type SingleSessionApi = {\r\n  info: () => Promise<EventInfo>;\r\n  slots: () => Promise<{ slots: SingleSessionSlot[] }>;\r\n  hold: (input: HoldInput) => Promise<HoldResult>;\r\n  status: (checkoutSessionId: string, bookingToken: string) => Promise<SingleSessionStatus>;\r\n  abandon: (checkoutSessionId: string, bookingToken: string) => Promise<{ status: string }>;\r\n  cancel: (cancelToken: string, confirm: boolean) => Promise<SingleSessionStatus>;\r\n  getTurnstileToken: () => Promise<string>;\r\n  redirectToCheckout: (url: string) => void;\r\n};\r\n\r\ntype PendingCheckout = HoldResult;\r\nconst PENDING_KEY = "lsatu.single-session.pending";\r\nconst IDEMPOTENCY_KEY = "lsatu.single-session.idempotency";\r\n// The single session keeps the original, unsuffixed keys so a hold already in\r\n// someone\'s browser survives this change. Other events get their own namespace\r\n// so a held consultation and a held session can never read each other\'s token.\r\nconst pendingKey = (event: string) =>\r\n  event === "single-session" ? PENDING_KEY : `${PENDING_KEY}.${event}`;\r\nconst idempotencyKeyFor = (event: string) =>\r\n  event === "single-session" ? IDEMPOTENCY_KEY : `${IDEMPOTENCY_KEY}.${event}`;\r\n\r\ndeclare global {\r\n  interface Window {\r\n    turnstile?: {\r\n      render: (element: HTMLElement, options: Record<string, unknown>) => string;\r\n      remove: (widgetId: string) => void;\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function invoke<T>(action: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<T> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("single-session", { body: { action, ...extra } });\r\n  if (error) {\r\n    // supabase-js turns any non-2xx into "Edge Function returned a non-2xx\r\n    // status code" and keeps the body on the error object. That body is where\r\n    // the reason lives -- unknown_event, link_already_used -- and it is the\r\n    // whole difference between "something went wrong" and "this link is spent".\r\n    const body = (error as { context?: { json?: () => Promise<{ error?: string }> } }).context;\r\n    const reason = await body?.json?.().then((parsed) => parsed?.error).catch(() => undefined);\r\n    throw new Error(reason || error.message);\r\n  }\r\n  const result = data as { error?: string } & T;\r\n  if (result?.error) throw new Error(result.error);\r\n  return result;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction turnstileToken(): Promise<string> {\r\n  const sitekey = import.meta.env.VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY as string | undefined;\r\n  if (!sitekey) return Promise.reject(new Error("security_check_unavailable"));\r\n  const run = () => new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {\r\n    if (!window.turnstile) return reject(new Error("security_check_unavailable"));\r\n    const mount = document.createElement("div");\r\n    mount.hidden = true;\r\n    document.body.appendChild(mount);\r\n    let widgetId = "";\r\n    const clean = () => {\r\n      if (widgetId) window.turnstile?.remove(widgetId);\r\n      mount.remove();\r\n    };\r\n    widgetId = window.turnstile.render(mount, {\r\n      sitekey,\r\n      size: "invisible",\r\n      callback: (token: string) => { clean(); resolve(token); },\r\n      "error-callback": () => { clean(); reject(new Error("security_check_failed")); },\r\n      "expired-callback": () => { clean(); reject(new Error("security_check_expired")); },\r\n    });\r\n  });\r\n  if (window.turnstile) return run();\r\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\r\n    const existing = document.querySelector<HTMLScriptElement>(\'script[data-lsatu-turnstile="true"]\');\r\n    const script = existing ?? document.createElement("script");\r\n    if (!existing) {\r\n      script.src = "https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js?render=explicit";\r\n      script.async = true;\r\n      script.defer = true;\r\n      script.dataset.lsatuTurnstile = "true";\r\n      document.head.appendChild(script);\r\n    }\r\n    script.addEventListener("load", () => void run().then(resolve, reject), { once: true });\r\n    script.addEventListener("error", () => reject(new Error("security_check_unavailable")), { once: true });\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * One api per event. Every call names the event it is about, so the same page\r\n * serves the paid session and the free consultation without either of them\r\n * being able to answer for the other.\r\n */\r\nexport function makeApi(eventSlug: string): SingleSessionApi {\r\n  return {\r\n    info: () => invoke("info", { event: eventSlug }),\r\n    slots: () => invoke("slots", { event: eventSlug }),\r\n    hold: (input) => invoke("hold", { ...input, event: eventSlug }),\r\n    status: (checkoutSessionId, bookingToken) => invoke("status", { event: eventSlug, checkout_session_id: checkoutSessionId, booking_token: bookingToken }),\r\n    abandon: (checkoutSessionId, bookingToken) => invoke("abandon", { event: eventSlug, checkout_session_id: checkoutSessionId, booking_token: bookingToken }),\r\n    cancel: (cancelToken, confirm) => invoke("cancel", { event: eventSlug, cancel_token: cancelToken, confirm }),\r\n    getTurnstileToken: turnstileToken,\r\n    redirectToCheckout: (url) => window.location.assign(url),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst defaultApi: SingleSessionApi = makeApi("single-session");\r\n\r\nfunction readPending(event = "single-session"): PendingCheckout | null {\r\n  try {\r\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(pendingKey(event)) ?? ## assistant

Backend audit complete. Main implementation findings were sent to the parent agent.

Key risks surfaced:

- Calendar confirmation emails are delayed because the worker claims jobs only once before calendar promotion unblocks them.
- Timezone and duration are currently hardcoded in the email worker.
- `schedule_events.html_link` already provides the correct calendar invitation URL.
- The status RPC can safely expose enriched confirmation data behind its existing booking capability token.
- Free consultations cannot currently poll calendar readiness because they lack a Stripe checkout session.
- The database test runner does not apply the latest booking migrations, so current-schema coverage must be extended.
- Separately, payment finalization and refunds still contain a hardcoded 4,500-cent amount, which can affect $1 one-off bookings.
